# Public bid-document harvest — 2026-10-09

Generated 2026-10-09T09:22:48Z. Public procurement records only; local review copies, no republication.

## Round totals

459 unique downloaded PDFs; 459 triaged; 2840149575 saved bytes; 2999029552 budgeted document/HTML bytes against 3000000000 bytes. Robots responses are separately bounded to 2 MiB and excluded from the document budget.

Caps: 150,000,000 bytes per file and 3,000,000,000 bytes per round (decimal MB/GB). Downloads and previews are ignored by git.

The first process used binary limits; it was interrupted before reaching either decimal limit, corrected, and resumed without resetting the consumed budget. 157286400 bytes were conservatively reserved for an interrupted transfer whose exact network byte count was unavailable. Already processed URLs were retained; the OA listing was re-read to reconstruct the remaining queue. Original limits and the resumption event are preserved in round.json.

## Complete candidate sets

These are marker-based classifications, not verified product-ready sets. Sheet IDs or specification references can occur in indexes or addenda; previews require visual review. Page indexes below are zero-based PDF ordinals; add one for the page number. Sampling of sets over 200 pages can miss markers.

## Visual review

a4b7b0a81c5a6602506b44e81baf18866d492adea8982830c3a9c229874d68af; reviewed 2026-10-09T09:19:45Z. Schedule/plan preview inspected by eye; hardware specification text inspected. Not a product acceptance test.

- door_schedule: page index 14, A-101. Actual door schedule: door marks 1–7, width/height, type, material, hardware codes and access-control/frame columns.
- floor_plan: page index 14, A-101. Actual demolition and proposed floor plans, including door locations and marks.
- hardware_spec: page index 19, A-801. 08710 Door Hardware; Hardware Group No. 01 and further inventories with quantities, descriptions, catalog numbers, finishes and manufacturers.

## Triage limits

Extraction uses pdftotext -layout independently per page. For sets over 200 pages: first 60, last 20 and each tenth intervening page. No OCR. No-text pages are scanned candidates (including possible blank/vector pages). All marker hits are retained, including reference-only pages. Classification and preview selection use qualified content: schedule row structure or standalone schedule headings with column headers; hardware section headings/footers with section body or items, or numbered set/group headings with hardware items; large plan sheets or wide scale/north-bearing layouts with a standalone floor-plan heading. Drawing indexes, contents, RFI lists and replace-drawing narratives are excluded; AIA form IDs are excluded from floor-plan content. Weak sheet IDs alone do not qualify content. Page dimensions come from pdfinfo. These checks remain heuristic and can miss scanned, sparse, unusual or unsampled sheets. All per-page scores, qualification flags, extraction errors and examined/scanned page indexes are in triage.json. Classification precedence is complete, pair, schedule-only, spec-only, plan-only, none.

The none class means no qualified requested content; it does not mean the PDF is invalid or lacks other useful plans. Site, grading, phasing, roof and other plan types can remain none under the requested floor-plan criteria. A separately supplied REVIEW-plans-none-2026-10-09.md reviews an earlier partial snapshot; its counts are not final-round counts, and its proposed broader plantype/fifth-page changes were not applied to this floor-plan/tenth-page workflow.

16886 pages examined; 61 sets sampled; 713 examined pages had no extracted text; 74 previews. Zero extraction/render failures recorded; Poppler structural warnings remain in the local logs.

## Historical re-triage (round 3)

The former generated totals included later downloads and have been replaced by the scoped round sections below. The original classification snapshot cannot be recovered from a later triage.json. Historical method notes are retained here.

Architectural and other plans have separate page/set flags. Complete requires architectural floor-plan content plus a door schedule and hardware content. Plan-only can contain either plan category. Title text (or a qualifying architectural title-block sheet ID when no title is extracted) is in content_evidence. MEP, ceiling, roof, phasing and site titles do not qualify as architectural merely because they contain FLOOR or OVERALL.

For sets over 200 pages, classification examines first 60, last 20 and every fifth intervening page. pdftotext runs once per PDF with form-feed page boundaries; only selected pages are classified. Unsampled pages are unknown. raster_only is the requested no-fonts proxy, not proof that all objects are raster. Door tag-shaped text can also be room numbers or drawing references. No OCR or credentials were used.

Generated 2026-10-10T13:36:39Z. All timestamps below are UTC from the recorded clock. Each round is the manifest interval from its start offset up to the next round's start (or the current manifest end), joined to triage.json by SHA256. Triage timestamps can fall after harvesting or change during re-triage.

Class counts use the current triage classifications of only the PDFs added in that round. Missing triage is reported separately. The historical text above describes its dated snapshot; the cumulative table below describes the current corpus.

Received bytes are the charged document/HTML transfer budget; saved bytes are downloaded PDFs. Robots bytes are excluded. Skipped-at-cap counts are manifest records, distinct from the final remaining queue, which can be changed by a later listing audit.
